public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        }
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        return -1;
    }

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```java
public class IsToeplitzMatrix {
    public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j < m; j++) {
                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures \result == (mat.length == 0 ? true : (\forall int i, j; 1 <= i && i < mat.length && 1 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]));

        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];

        //@ decreases n - i;

            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;

            //@ loop_writes \nothing;

    }

    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures (\forall int i, j; 1 < i && i < mat.length && 1 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);  
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 1 <= k && k < j; mat[i][k] == mat[i - 1][k - 1];
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;

                }

    }

public class IsToeplitzMatrix {
    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 1 < i && i <= mat.length || 1 <= j && j <= mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);  
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 1 <= k && k < j; mat[i][k] == mat[i - 1][k - 1];
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases m - j;
            for (int j = 1; j < m; j++) {
                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

public class IsToeplitzMatrix {
    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 1 <= i && i <= mat.length && 1 <= j || j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);  
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 1 <= k && k < j; mat[i][k] == mat[i - 1][k - 1];
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases m - j;
            for (int j = 1; j < m; j++) {
                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 1 - 1 <= i && i < mat.length && 1 < j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);

        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 1 <= k && k < j; mat[i][k] == mat[i - 1][k - 1];

                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*@ spec_public @*/ public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        //@ requires mat != null;
        //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < mat.length && 0 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i] != null && mat[i].length == mat[0].length);
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 1 - 1 <= i && i <= mat.length && 1 <= j && j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);  
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, j; 1 <= k && k < i && 1 <= j && j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j && j <= m;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 1 <= k && k < j; mat[i][k] == mat[i - 1][k - 1];
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases m - j;

                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
